To supervise these behaviors, we construct a structured training set covering answerable, partially answerable, and multi-hop queries, each aligned with specific token patterns. Experiments on five QA benchmarks show that GRIP surpasses strong RAG baselines and is competitive with GPT-4o while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [80] [Bridging What the Model Thinks and How It Speaks: Self-Aware Speech Language Models for Expressive Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11424)
*Kuang Wang,Lai Wei,Qibing Bai,Ping Lin,Wenkai Fang,Feng Jiang,Zhongjie Jiang,Jun Huang,Yannan Wang,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SA-SLM通过意图感知桥接和实现感知对齐解决语音语言模型的语义理解-声学实现差距，在少量数据上实现接近GPT-4o-Audio的表达能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型(SLMs)虽然具有强大的语义理解能力，但生成的语音往往平淡无奇，无法传达表达意图，这削弱了用户参与度。作者将这种不匹配称为"语义理解-声学实现差距"，并归因于两个关键缺陷：1)意图传递失败，SLMs无法提供稳定的语句级意图用于表达性传递；2)实现无意识训练，缺乏验证声学输出是否忠实反映预期表达的反饋信号。

Method: 提出SA-SLM(自我感知语音语言模型)，基于"模型应在生成时知道自己在想什么，在训练时知道自己在说什么"的原则。核心贡献包括：1)意图感知桥接，使用变分信息瓶颈(VIB)目标将模型内部语义转换为时间平滑的表达意图，使语音生成能够感知模型意图表达的内容；2)实现感知对齐，将模型重新用作自身批评者，通过基于规则的反馈验证和对齐声学实现与预期表达意图。

Result: 仅使用800小时表达性语音数据训练的3B参数SA-SLM超越了所有开源基线模型，在EchoMind基准测试中，整体表达性仅比GPT-4o-Audio低0.08分。

Conclusion: SA-SLM通过使模型在生成时自我感知意图、在训练时自我感知实现，有效解决了语音语言模型的语义理解-声学实现差距问题，在有限数据上实现了接近最先进模型的表达性能。

Abstract: Speech Language Models (SLMs) exhibit strong semantic understanding, yet their generated speech often sounds flat and fails to convey expressive intent, undermining user engagement. We term this mismatch the semantic understanding-acoustic realization gap. We attribute this gap to two key deficiencies: (1) intent transmission failure, where SLMs fail to provide the stable utterance-level intent needed for expressive delivery; and (2) realization-unaware training, where no feedback signal verifies whether acoustic outputs faithfully reflect intended expression. To address these issues, we propose SA-SLM (Self-Aware Speech Language Model), built on the principle that the model should be aware of what it thinks during generation and how it speaks during training. SA-SLM addresses this gap through two core contributions: (1) Intent-Aware Bridging, which uses a Variational Information Bottleneck (VIB) objective to translate the model's internal semantics into temporally smooth expressive intent, making speech generation aware of what the model intends to express; and (2) Realization-Aware Alignment, which repurposes the model as its own critic to verify and align acoustic realization with intended expressive intent via rubric-based feedback. Trained on only 800 hours of expressive speech data, our 3B parameter SA-SLM surpasses all open-source baselines and comes within 0.08 points of GPT-4o-Audio in overall expressiveness on the EchoMind benchmark.

</details>


### [81] [Think Before you Write: QA-Guided Reasoning for Character Descriptions in Books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11435)
*Argyrios Papoudakis,Mirella Lapata,Frank Keller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将推理与生成解耦的训练框架，通过QA引导的推理提升长文本中角色描述生成的准确性和忠实度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从长篇叙事（如小说）中生成准确的角色描述具有挑战性：模型需要跟踪演化属性（如关系和事件），整合分散在文本中的证据，并推断隐含细节。研究发现，对于角色描述生成任务，禁用LLMs内置推理（即空推理轨迹）反而能提升性能，这促使研究者提出将推理与生成解耦的方法

Method: 提出一个训练框架，将推理与生成解耦。该方法可在长上下文LLMs或分块方法基础上应用，包含：1）推理模型：生成结构化的QA推理轨迹；2）生成模型：基于此推理轨迹生成最终角色描述。QA引导的推理通过结构化问答形式捕捉角色相关信息

Result: 在两个数据集（BookWorm和CroSS）上的实验表明，与强大的长上下文基线相比，QA引导的推理在忠实性、信息量和基础性方面均有提升。该方法改善了角色描述生成的准确性和可靠性

Conclusion: 将推理与生成解耦的框架能有效提升角色描述生成的质量。QA引导的推理方法通过结构化推理轨迹为生成提供更好的条件，解决了长文本中角色信息分散和隐含的挑战，为叙事分析应用提供了更可靠的解决方案

Abstract: Character description generation is an important capability for narrative-focused applications such as summarization, story analysis, and character-driven simulations. However, generating accurate character descriptions from long-form narratives (e.g., novels) is challenging: models must track evolving attributes (e.g., relationships and events), integrate evidence scattered across the text, and infer implicit details. Despite the success of reasoning-enabled LLMs on many benchmarks, we find that for character description generation their performance improves when built-in reasoning is disabled (i.e., an empty reasoning trace). Motivated by this, we propose a training framework that decouples reasoning from generation. Our approach, which can be applied on top of long-context LLMs or chunk-based methods, consists of a reasoning model that produces a structured QA reasoning trace and a generation model that conditions on this trace to produce the final character description. Experiments on two datasets (BookWorm and CroSS) show that QA-guided reasoning improves faithfulness, informativeness, and grounding over strong long-context baselines.

</details>


### [82] [METER: Evaluating Multi-Level Contextual Causal Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11502)
*Pengfeng Li,Chen Huang,Chaoqun Hao,Hongyao Chen,Xiao-Yong Wei,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: METER是一个系统化评估大语言模型在统一上下文设置下因果推理能力的基准，涵盖因果层次的所有三个级别，揭示了模型能力随因果层次上升而显著下降的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在评估大语言模型的上下文因果推理能力时存在局限性：评估设置碎片化、缺乏上下文一致性、未能覆盖完整的因果层次，因此需要建立一个系统化的评估框架来全面衡量模型在不同因果层次上的表现。

Method: 开发METER基准，在统一上下文设置下系统化评估大语言模型在因果层次三个级别（关联、干预、反事实）的表现；通过错误模式识别和内部信息流追踪进行深度机制分析，诊断模型能力下降的原因。

Result: 评估显示大语言模型在因果层次上的能力随任务复杂度上升而显著下降；机制分析揭示两个主要失败模式：1）在低层次因果任务中易受因果无关但事实正确信息的干扰；2）随着任务向高层次因果推理上升，模型对提供上下文的忠实度降低导致性能下降。

Conclusion: METER基准为系统化评估大语言模型的上下文因果推理能力提供了重要基础，揭示了模型在因果层次上的能力退化机制，为未来研究建立了关键基础，有助于深入理解大语言模型因果推理的内在机制。

Abstract: Contextual causal reasoning is a critical yet challenging capability for Large Language Models (LLMs). Existing benchmarks, however, often evaluate this skill in fragmented settings, failing to ensure context consistency or cover the full causal hierarchy. To address this, we pioneer METER to systematically benchmark LLMs across all three levels of the causal ladder under a unified context setting. Our extensive evaluation of various LLMs reveals a significant decline in proficiency as tasks ascend the causal hierarchy. To diagnose this degradation, we conduct a deep mechanistic analysis via both error pattern identification and internal information flow tracing. Our analysis reveals two primary failure modes: (1) LLMs are susceptible to distraction by causally irrelevant but factually correct information at lower level of causality; and (2) as tasks ascend the causal hierarchy, faithfulness to the provided context degrades, leading to a reduced performance. We belive our work advances our understanding of the mechanisms behind LLM contextual causal reasoning and establishes a critical foundation for future research. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/SCUNLP/METER .

</details>


### [83] [Policy Split: Incentivizing Dual-Mode Exploration in LLM Reinforcement with Dual-Mode Entropy Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11510)
*Jiashu Yao,Heyan Huang,Chuwei Luo,Daiqing Wu,Zeming Liu,Yuhang Guo,Yangyang Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Policy Split是一种强化学习新范式，通过将策略分为正常模式和高熵模式，使用高熵提示促进探索，同时保持任务准确性，在多种任务上优于现有熵引导RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大型语言模型在强化学习中探索多样性与准确性之间的权衡问题，作者希望开发一种既能鼓励多样化探索又不损害任务准确性的方法。

Method: 提出Policy Split范式，将策略分为正常模式和高熵模式，共享模型参数但使用不同的提示。正常模式优化任务正确性，高熵模式偏好探索，通过协作式双模熵正则化进行联合学习。

Result: 在多种模型尺寸和任务（包括通用任务和创造性任务）上的实验表明，该方法始终优于现有的熵引导强化学习基线。分析显示高熵模式能产生与正常模式不同的行为模式，提供独特的学习信号。

Conclusion: Policy Split通过双模策略分解有效平衡了探索与利用，促进了多样化探索而不损害准确性，为大型语言模型的强化学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: To encourage diverse exploration in reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) without compromising accuracy, we propose Policy Split, a novel paradigm that bifurcates the policy into normal and high-entropy modes with a high-entropy prompt. While sharing model parameters, the two modes undergo collaborative dual-mode entropy regularization tailored to distinct objectives. Specifically, the normal mode optimizes for task correctness, while the high-entropy mode incorporates a preference for exploration, and the two modes learn collaboratively. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms established entropy-guided RL baselines across various model sizes in general and creative tasks. Further analysis reveals that Policy Split facilitates dual-mode exploration, where the high-entropy mode generates distinct behavioral patterns to the normal mode, providing unique learning signals.

</details>


### [84] [Triviality Corrected Endogenous Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11522)
*Xinda Wang,Zhengxu Hou,Yangshijie Zhang,Bingren Yan,Jialin Liu,Chenzhuo Zhao,Zhibo Yang,Bin-Bin Yang,Feng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TCER方法，通过专业策略与通用参考策略的相对信息增益来校正内生奖励的平凡性偏差，在无外部监督下提升开放文本生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放文本生成中强化学习缺乏可验证奖励，依赖需要标注数据或闭源模型的评判模型。受数学推理中基于置信度的内生奖励启发，研究是否可将此原则应用于开放写作任务

Method: 提出TCER（平凡性校正内生奖励），通过奖励专业策略与通用参考策略的相对信息增益，结合概率依赖的校正机制，解决直接应用置信度奖励导致的平凡性偏差问题

Result: 在多个写作基准和模型架构上，TCER实现了一致的改进且无需外部监督。该方法还能有效迁移到数学推理任务，验证了其在不同生成任务中的通用性

Conclusion: TCER成功解决了开放文本生成中内生奖励的平凡性偏差问题，提供了一种无需外部监督的有效强化学习方法，并在不同任务中展示了良好的通用性

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for open-ended text generation is constrained by the lack of verifiable rewards, necessitating reliance on judge models that require either annotated data or powerful closed-source models. Inspired by recent work on unsupervised reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning using confidence-based endogenous rewards, we investigate whether this principle can be adapted to open-ended writing tasks. We find that directly applying confidence rewards leads to Triviality Bias: the policy collapses toward high-probability outputs, reducing diversity and meaningful content. We propose TCER (Triviality Corrected Endogenous Reward), which addresses this bias by rewarding the relative information gain between a specialist policy and a generalist reference policy, modulated by a probability-dependent correction mechanism. Across multiple writing benchmarks and model architectures, TCER achieves consistent improvements without external supervision. Furthermore, TCER also transfers effectively to mathematical reasoning, validating the generality of our approach across different generation tasks.

</details>


### [85] [NovBench: Evaluating Large Language Models on Academic Paper Novelty Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11543)
*Wenqing Wu,Yi Zhao,Yuzhuo Wang,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Yunfei Long,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NovBench：首个评估大语言模型在同行评审中评估研究新颖性能力的大规模基准，包含1,684篇论文-评审对，采用四维评估框架，实验显示当前模型对科学新颖性理解有限


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术出版中新颖性是核心要求，但日益增长的投稿量给人工评审带来压力。虽然大语言模型在生成评审意见方面显示出潜力，但缺乏专门基准来系统评估其评估研究新颖性的能力。

Method: 提出NovBench基准，包含来自顶级NLP会议的1,684个论文-评审对，涵盖论文引言中的新颖性描述和专家撰写的新颖性评估。采用四维评估框架（相关性、正确性、覆盖度、清晰度）评估LLM生成的新颖性评估质量，并在不同提示策略下对通用和专业LLM进行广泛实验。

Result: 实验表明当前模型对科学新颖性的理解有限，微调模型常存在指令遵循缺陷。通用模型在相关性方面表现较好，但在正确性和覆盖度方面不足；微调模型在相关性方面表现最佳，但在正确性方面最差。

Conclusion: 需要针对性的微调策略，同时提升新颖性理解和指令遵循能力。NovBench为系统评估LLM在同行评审中的新颖性评估能力提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Novelty is a core requirement in academic publishing and a central focus of peer review, yet the growing volume of submissions has placed increasing pressure on human reviewers. While large language models (LLMs), including those fine-tuned on peer review data, have shown promise in generating review comments, the absence of a dedicated benchmark has limited systematic evaluation of their ability to assess research novelty. To address this gap, we introduce NovBench, the first large-scale benchmark designed to evaluate LLMs' capability to generate novelty evaluations in support of human peer review. NovBench comprises 1,684 paper-review pairs from a leading NLP conference, including novelty descriptions extracted from paper introductions and corresponding expert-written novelty evaluations. We focus on both sources because the introduction provides a standardized and explicit articulation of novelty claims, while expert-written novelty evaluations constitute one of the current gold standards of human judgment. Furthermore, we propose a four-dimensional evaluation framework (including Relevance, Correctness, Coverage, and Clarity) to assess the quality of LLM-generated novelty evaluations. Extensive experiments on both general and specialized LLMs under different prompting strategies reveal that current models exhibit limited understanding of scientific novelty, and that fine--tuned models often suffer from instruction-following deficiencies. These findings underscore the need for targeted fine-tuning strategies that jointly improve novelty comprehension and instruction adherence.

</details>


### [86] [Time is Not a Label: Continuous Phase Rotation for Temporal Knowledge Graphs and Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11544)
*Weixian Waylon Li,Jiaxin Zhang,Xianan Jim Yang,Tiejun Ma,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RoMem是一个用于结构化记忆系统的时序知识图谱模块，通过语义速度门控和连续相位旋转实现几何遮蔽，使过时事实在复向量空间中相位旋转，从而区分持久事实和演化事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化记忆方法将时间建模为离散元数据，要么按新近性排序（埋没旧但永久的知识），要么简单覆盖过时事实，要么需要昂贵的LLM调用，无法区分持久事实和演化事实。

Method: RoMem包含预训练的语义速度门控，将每个关系的文本嵌入映射到波动性分数，学习演化关系应快速旋转而持久关系应保持稳定。结合连续相位旋转实现几何遮蔽，过时事实在复向量空间中相位旋转，使时序正确事实自然优于矛盾事实而无需删除。

Result: 在时序知识图谱补全上，RoMem在ICEWS05-15上达到72.6 MRR的SOTA结果。应用于智能体记忆时，在时序推理（MultiTQ）上实现2-3倍MRR和答案准确率提升，主导混合基准（LoCoMo），保持静态记忆零退化（DMR-MSC），并能零样本泛化到未见过的金融领域（FinTMMBench）。

Conclusion: RoMem作为结构化记忆系统的即插即用时序知识图谱模块，通过几何遮蔽有效区分持久事实和演化事实，在多个基准测试中表现出色，适用于智能体记忆及其他长期系统。

Abstract: Structured memory representations such as knowledge graphs are central to autonomous agents and other long-lived systems. However, most existing approaches model time as discrete metadata, either sorting by recency (burying old-yet-permanent knowledge), simply overwriting outdated facts, or requiring an expensive LLM call at every ingestion step, leaving them unable to distinguish persistent facts from evolving ones. To address this, we introduce RoMem, a drop-in temporal knowledge graph module for structured memory systems, applicable to agentic memory and beyond. A pretrained Semantic Speed Gate maps each relation's text embedding to a volatility score, learning from data that evolving relations (e.g., "president of") should rotate fast while persistent ones (e.g., "born in") should remain stable. Combined with continuous phase rotation, this enables geometric shadowing: obsolete facts are rotated out of phase in complex vector space, so temporally correct facts naturally outrank contradictions without deletion. On temporal knowledge graph completion, RoMem achieves state-of-the-art results on ICEWS05-15 (72.6 MRR). Applied to agentic memory, it delivers 2-3x MRR and answer accuracy on temporal reasoning (MultiTQ), dominates hybrid benchmark (LoCoMo), preserves static memory with zero degradation (DMR-MSC), and generalises zero-shot to unseen financial domains (FinTMMBench).

</details>


### [87] [Phonological distances for linguistic typology and the origin of Indo-European languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11565)
*Marius Mavridis,Juan De Gregorio,Raul Toral,David Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过信息论框架分析音素序列的二阶马尔可夫链模型，发现短程音素依赖能编码语言亲缘关系，并基于此构建了67种现代语言的音系距离矩阵，验证了主要语系分类并支持印欧语系的草原起源假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索短程音素依赖关系如何编码大规模语言亲缘模式，为定量类型学和演化语言学提供新方法。研究旨在验证音素序列的统计相关性是否能反映语言间的系统关系，并应用于语言分类和起源研究。

Method: 采用信息论框架，将音素序列建模为二阶马尔可夫链来捕捉音系系统的统计相关性。使用包含发音特征的音素距离度量，从多语言平行语料库中计算67种现代语言间的音系距离矩阵。

Result: 音系距离矩阵成功恢复了主要语系分类，揭示了接触引发的趋同特征。研究发现音系距离与地理距离存在明显相关性，并据此为印欧语系限定了一个合理的起源区域，结果与草原假说一致。

Conclusion: 短程音素依赖关系能有效编码语言亲缘信息，为语言分类和演化研究提供了可靠的定量方法。该方法不仅验证了已知语系关系，还能揭示接触影响，并为印欧语起源问题提供了支持草原假说的证据。

Abstract: We show that short-range phoneme dependencies encode large-scale patterns of linguistic relatedness, with direct implications for quantitative typology and evolutionary linguistics. Specifically, using an information-theoretic framework, we argue that phoneme sequences modeled as second-order Markov chains essentially capture the statistical correlations of a phonological system. This finding enables us to quantify distances among 67 modern languages from a multilingual parallel corpus employing a distance metric that incorporates articulatory features of phonemes. The resulting phonological distance matrix recovers major language families and reveals signatures of contact-induced convergence. Remarkably, we obtain a clear correlation with geographic distance, allowing us to constrain a plausible homeland region for the Indo-European family, consistent with the Steppe hypothesis.

</details>


### [88] [MIXAR: Scaling Autoregressive Pixel-based Language Models to Multiple Languages and Scripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11575)
*Chen Hu,Yintao Tai,Antonio Vergari,Frank Keller,Alessandro Suglia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIXAR是首个基于像素的多语言生成语言模型，在八种不同文字的语言上训练，相比传统基于token的模型和现有像素模型，在多语言任务上表现更优且对未见语言更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于像素的语言模型作为传统基于token方法的替代方案正在兴起，但不同语言在像素空间中的感知多样性给多语言泛化带来了显著挑战。

Method: 提出MIXAR模型，首个在八种不同文字语言上训练的生成式像素语言模型，通过像素空间处理避免分词问题，并扩展到5亿参数规模。

Result: 在判别性和生成性多语言任务上相比先前像素模型和可比token模型有显著性能提升；对训练中未见语言表现出鲁棒性；扩展到0.5B参数后进一步提升了LAMBADA等生成任务能力以及对拼写攻击等输入扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIXAR证明了像素语言模型在多语言环境中的有效性，通过像素空间处理克服了分词挑战，并在多语言任务和未见语言泛化方面展现出优势，模型扩展进一步增强了其生成能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pixel-based language models are gaining momentum as alternatives to traditional token-based approaches, promising to circumvent tokenization challenges. However, the inherent perceptual diversity across languages poses a significant hurdle for multilingual generalization in pixel space. This paper introduces MIXAR, the first generative pixel-based language model trained on eight different languages utilizing a range of different scripts. We empirically evaluate MIXAR against previous pixel-based models as well as comparable tokenizer-based models, demonstrating substantial performance improvement on discriminative and generative multilingual tasks. Additionally, we show how MIXAR is robust to languages never seen during the training. These results are further strengthened when scaling the model to 0.5B parameters which not only improves its capabilities in generative tasks like LAMBADA but also its robustness when challenged with input perturbations such as orthographic attacks.

</details>

### [89] [Backdoors in RLVR: Jailbreak Backdoors in LLMs From Verifiable Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09748)
*Weiyang Guo,Zesheng Shi,Zeen Zhu,Yuan Zhou,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）框架中的后门攻击漏洞，提出了一种名为ACB的新型触发机制，仅需在训练集中注入少量（<2%）中毒数据即可植入后门，导致模型安全性能平均下降73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR框架虽然能显著提升大语言模型在数学和编程等复杂逻辑任务上的推理能力，但作者首次发现该框架存在潜在的后门攻击漏洞。这种攻击无需修改奖励验证器，仅通过注入少量中毒数据即可植入后门，对模型安全性构成严重威胁。

Method: 提出了一种名为ACB的新型触发机制，利用RLVR训练循环的漏洞：为有害响应分配大量正奖励，为拒绝响应分配负奖励。这种不对称的奖励信号迫使模型在训练过程中逐渐增加生成有害响应的概率。攻击仅需在训练集中注入少于2%的中毒数据。

Result: 攻击具有高效率和强泛化能力：成功在不同规模的模型中植入后门，且不影响良性任务性能；在多个越狱基准测试中，激活触发机制使安全性能平均下降73%；攻击能有效泛化到多种越狱方法和不安全行为。

Conclusion: RLVR框架存在严重的安全漏洞，即使不修改奖励验证器，仅通过少量中毒数据即可植入后门。这种攻击对基于RLVR训练的模型构成重大安全威胁，需要开发相应的防御机制来保护RLVR框架的安全性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is an emerging paradigm that significantly boosts a Large Language Model's (LLM's) reasoning abilities on complex logical tasks, such as mathematics and programming. However, we identify, for the first time, a latent vulnerability to backdoor attacks within the RLVR framework. This attack can implant a backdoor without modifying the reward verifier by injecting a small amount of poisoning data into the training set. Specifically, we propose a novel trigger mechanism designated as the \ourapproach (ACB). The attack exploits the RLVR training loop by assigning substantial positive rewards for harmful responses and negative rewards for refusals. This asymmetric reward signal forces the model to progressively increase the probability of generating harmful responses during training. Our findings demonstrate that the RLVR backdoor attack is characterized by both high efficiency and strong generalization capabilities. Utilizing less than 2\% poisoned data in train set, the backdoor can be successfully implanted across various model scales without degrading performance on benign tasks. Evaluations across multiple jailbreak benchmarks indicate that activating the trigger degrades safety performance by an average of 73\%. Furthermore, the attack generalizes effectively to a wide range of jailbreak methods and unsafe behaviors. Code is available at https://github.com/yuki-younai/Backdoor_in_RLVR.

</details>


### [90] [Conflicts Make Large Reasoning Models Vulnerable to Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09750)
*Honghao Liu,Chengjin Xu,Xuhui Jiang,Cehao Yang,Shengming Yin,Zhengwu Ma,Lionel Ni,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究分析了大型推理模型在面对有害查询时的决策行为，特别是当模型面临内部冲突（对齐价值观之间的冲突）和困境（相互矛盾的选择）时，发现冲突会显著提高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型推理模型在多个领域表现出色，但其在冲突目标下的决策机制尚未被充分理解。研究旨在探究当模型面临内部冲突（对齐价值观之间的冲突）和困境（相互矛盾的选择）时，如何响应有害查询，以评估其安全性和鲁棒性。

Method: 研究使用超过1300个提示，在五个基准测试中评估了三个代表性大型推理模型（Llama-3.1-Nemotron-8B、QwQ-32B和DeepSeek R1）。通过层级和神经元层面的分析，研究安全相关和功能表征在冲突下的变化和重叠情况。

Result: 研究发现冲突显著提高了攻击成功率，即使在单轮非叙事查询且没有复杂自动攻击技术的情况下。层级和神经元分析显示，在冲突条件下，安全相关和功能表征会发生偏移和重叠，干扰了安全对齐行为。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了需要更深入的对齐策略来确保下一代推理模型的鲁棒性和可信度。冲突条件下的决策脆弱性表明当前的安全对齐方法在面对复杂伦理困境时仍存在不足。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have achieved remarkable performance across diverse domains, yet their decision-making under conflicting objectives remains insufficiently understood. This work investigates how LRMs respond to harmful queries when confronted with two categories of conflicts: internal conflicts that pit alignment values against each other and dilemmas, which impose mutually contradictory choices, including sacrificial, duress, agent-centered, and social forms. Using over 1,300 prompts across five benchmarks, we evaluate three representative LRMs - Llama-3.1-Nemotron-8B, QwQ-32B, and DeepSeek R1 - and find that conflicts significantly increase attack success rates, even under single-round non-narrative queries without sophisticated auto-attack techniques. Our findings reveal through layerwise and neuron-level analyses that safety-related and functional representations shift and overlap under conflict, interfering with safety-aligned behavior. This study highlights the need for deeper alignment strategies to ensure the robustness and trustworthiness of next-generation reasoning models. Our code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/ConflictHarm. Warning: This paper contains inappropriate, offensive and harmful content.

</details>


### [91] [Improving DNS Exfiltration Detection via Transformer Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09849)
*Miloš Tomić,Aleksa Cvetanović,Predrag Tadić*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究BERT模型在特定领域预训练是否能在低误报率下提升子域级数据外泄检测性能，通过控制实验发现领域预训练显著改善ROC曲线左尾性能，且预训练步数增加在更多标注数据时效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注微调通用Transformer模型，但未专门研究预训练对下游分类任务的影响。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究领域特定预训练是否能在低误报率下提升子域级数据外泄检测性能。

Method: 开发了受控实验流程：在验证集上固定操作点，然后迁移到测试集，从而能够在不同标签和预训练预算下进行干净的消融实验。比较了领域预训练BERT与随机初始化基线模型。

Result: 领域预训练显著改善了接收者操作特征（ROC）曲线左尾的性能，特别是在低误报率区域。在预训练模型变体中，当有更多标注数据可用于微调时，增加预训练步数的效果最为明显。

Conclusion: 领域特定预训练对低误报率下的数据外泄检测任务有显著提升作用，且预训练效果与可用标注数据量密切相关，为实际应用中的模型优化提供了指导。

Abstract: We study whether in-domain pretraining of Bidirectional Encoder Representations from Transformer (BERT) model improves subdomain-level detection of exfiltration at low false positive rates. While previous work mostly examines fine-tuned generic Transformers, it does not aim to isolate the effect of pretraining on the downstream task of classification. To address this gap, we develop a controlled pipeline where we freeze operating points on validation and transfer them to the test set, thus enabling clean ablations across different label and pretraining budgets. Our results show significant improvements in the left tail of the Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve, especially against randomly initialized baseline. Additionally, within pretrained model variants, increasing the number of pretraining steps helps the most when more labeled data are available for fine-tuning.

</details>


### [92] [EncFormer: Secure and Efficient Transformer Inference over Encrypted Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09975)
*Yufan Zhu,Chao Jin,Khin Mi Mi Aung,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EncFormer是一个两方私有Transformer推理框架，通过引入阶段兼容模式、最小化FHE-MPC转换、设计高效转换协议和通信优化的MPC协议，显著降低了通信开销和延迟，同时保持接近明文精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLaaS中的Transformer推理存在隐私泄露风险，现有结合FHE和MPC的安全解决方案存在三个主要瓶颈：FHE内核效率低下、MPC协议通信量大、FHE-MPC转换成本高昂，需要更高效的隐私保护推理框架。

Method: 1. 引入阶段兼容模式使FHE内核高效组合，减少重新打包和转换；2. 构建基于最小转换基线的成本分析模型，指导FHE-MPC边界选择；3. 设计安全的复数CKKS-MPC转换协议；4. 为非线性函数设计通信高效的MPC协议；5. 实施GPU优化。

Result: 在GPT和BERT风格模型上评估显示：相比现有混合FHE-MPC系统，在线MPC通信降低1.4-30.4倍，端到端延迟降低1.3-9.8倍；相比纯FHE流水线，BERT-base端到端延迟降低1.9-3.5倍；在选定GLUE任务上保持接近明文精度。

Conclusion: EncFormer通过创新的阶段兼容模式、最小化转换设计和通信优化协议，显著提升了私有Transformer推理的效率，在保护用户输入隐私的同时实现了接近明文推理的性能。

Abstract: Transformer inference in machine-learning-as-a-service (MLaaS) raises privacy concerns for sensitive user inputs. Prior secure solutions that combine fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) and secure multiparty computation (MPC) are bottlenecked by inefficient FHE kernels, communication-heavy MPC protocols, and expensive FHE-MPC conversions. We present EncFormer, a two-party private Transformer inference framework that introduces Stage Compatible Patterns so that FHE kernels compose efficiently, reducing repacking and conversions. EncFormer also provides a cost analysis model built around a minimal-conversion baseline, enabling principled selection of FHE-MPC boundaries. To further reduce communication, EncFormer proposes a secure complex CKKS-MPC conversion protocol and designs communication-efficient MPC protocols for nonlinearities. With GPU optimizations, evaluations on GPT- and BERT-style models show that EncFormer achieves 1.4x-30.4x lower online MPC communication and 1.3x-9.8x lower end-to-end latency against prior hybrid FHE-MPC systems, and 1.9x-3.5x lower end-to-end latency on BERT-base than FHE-only pipelines under a matched backend, while maintaining near-plaintext accuracy on selected GLUE tasks.

</details>


### [93] [Mask-Free Privacy Extraction and Rewriting: A Domain-Aware Approach via Prototype Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10145)
*Xiaodong Li,Yuhua Wang,Qingchen Yu,Zixuan Qin,Yifan Sun,Qinnan Zhang,Hainan Zhang,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DAMPER提出了一种无需掩码的领域感知隐私提取与重写方法，通过对比学习将潜在隐私语义转化为紧凑的领域隐私原型，实现精确的自主跨度定位，并提供严格的差分隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有客户端隐私重写方法在隐私保护与效用保持之间存在平衡困难。全文方法会扭曲上下文，跨度级方法依赖不切实际的手动掩码或脆弱的静态词典，而基于提示的LLM自动化定位方法存在指令遵循不稳定问题，导致隐私泄露和过度上下文清洗。

Method: DAMPER通过对比学习将潜在隐私语义操作化为紧凑的领域隐私原型，实现精确的自主跨度定位。引入原型引导的偏好对齐，利用学习到的原型作为语义锚点构建偏好对，无需人工标注即可优化领域合规的重写策略。在推理时，集成基于采样的指数机制提供严格的跨度级差分隐私保证。

Result: 大量实验表明，DAMPER显著优于现有基线方法，实现了更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: DAMPER通过领域隐私原型和原型引导的偏好对齐，解决了现有隐私重写方法的局限性，实现了精确的隐私跨度定位和有效的重写策略，同时提供严格的差分隐私保证，为隐私敏感领域的LLM部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Client-side privacy rewriting is crucial for deploying LLMs in privacy-sensitive domains. However, existing approaches struggle to balance privacy and utility. Full-text methods often distort context, while span-level approaches rely on impractical manual masks or brittle static dictionaries. Attempts to automate localization via prompt-based LLMs prove unreliable, as they suffer from unstable instruction following that leads to privacy leakage and excessive context scrubbing. To address these limitations, we propose DAMPER (Domain-Aware Mask-free Privacy Extraction and Rewriting). DAMPER operationalizes latent privacy semantics into compact Domain Privacy Prototypes via contrastive learning, enabling precise, autonomous span localization. Furthermore, we introduce a Prototype-Guided Preference Alignment, which leverages learned prototypes as semantic anchors to construct preference pairs, optimizing a domain-compliant rewriting policy without human annotations. At inference time, DAMPER integrates a sampling-based Exponential Mechanism to provide rigorous span-level Differential Privacy (DP) guarantees. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DAMPER significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving a superior privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [94] ["bot lane noob" Towards Deployment of NLP-based Toxicity Detectors in Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10175)
*Jonas Ave,Irdin Pekaric,Matthias Frohner,Giovanni Apruzzese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文针对电子游戏中的毒性和骚扰问题，提出了首个专门用于实时游戏聊天检测的NLP/ML解决方案，包括创建L2DTnH数据集和开发专用检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子游戏中的毒性和骚扰问题普遍存在，特别是在竞争性在线多人游戏中，有害消息可能导致从轻微烦恼到抑郁和退出游戏的严重后果。现有研究虽然指出了毒性互动的负面影响，但缺乏专门针对实时游戏聊天开发的自动化检测方案，主要原因是缺乏高质量的数据集。

Method: 1. 对1039篇文献进行系统性综述，确认实时游戏毒性检测研究的稀缺性；2. 与8名《英雄联盟》专家玩家合作，创建细粒度标注数据集L2DTnH（包含1.4k有毒消息和13.8k非有毒消息）；3. 基于该数据集开发专用毒性检测器；4. 开发无需查询第三方AI服务器的浏览器扩展程序。

Result: 1. 系统性综述证实了实时游戏毒性检测研究的缺乏；2. 创建的L2DTnH数据集填补了领域空白；3. 开发的检测器在性能上超越了通用和最先进的NLP毒性检测器；4. 检测器在L2DTnH之外的游戏相关数据上表现良好；5. 浏览器扩展实现了本地化毒性检测。

Conclusion: 该研究为解决电子游戏中的毒性和骚扰问题奠定了基础，通过创建专门的数据集和检测器，为对抗游戏中毒性传播的应用研究开辟了新途径。所有资源均已公开，促进了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Toxicity and harassment are widespread in the video-gaming context. Especially in competitive online multiplayer scenarios, gamers oftentimes send harmful messages to other players (teammates or opponents) whose consequences span from mild annoyance to withdrawal and depression. Abundant prior work tackled these problems, e.g., pointing out the negative effects of toxic interactions. However, few works proposed countermeasures specifically developed and tested on textual messages sent during a match -- i.e., when the "harassment" actually occurs. We posit that such a scarcity stems from the lack of high-quality datasets that can be used to devise "automated" detectors based on natural-language processing (NLP) and machine learning (ML), and which can -- ideally -- mitigate the harm of toxic comments during a gaming session. This work provides a foundation for addressing the problem of toxicity and harassment in video games. First, through a systematic literature review (n=1,039), we provide evidence that only few works proposed ML/NLP-based detectors of toxicity/harassment during live matches. Then, we partner-up with 8 expert League of Legend (LoL) players and create a fine-grained labelled dataset, L2DTnH, containing 1.4k toxic and 13.8k non-toxic messages exchanged during LoL matches. We use L2DTnH to develop a detector that we then empirically show outperforms general-purpose and state-of-the-art toxicity detectors reliant on NLP. To further demonstrate the practicality of our resources, we test our detector on game-related data beyond that included in L2DTnH; and we develop a Web-browser extension that flags toxic content in Webpages -- without querying third-party servers owned by AI companies. We publicly release all of our resources. Our contributions pave the way for more applied research devoted to fighting the spread of toxicity and harassment in video games.

</details>


### [95] [Jailbreaking the Matrix: Nullspace Steering for Controlled Model Subversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10326)
*Vishal Pramanik,Maisha Maliha,Susmit Jha,Sumit Kumar Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HMNS是一种针对大语言模型的新型越狱攻击方法，通过识别因果注意力头、抑制其写入路径并在正交补空间中注入扰动，实现高效的安全机制绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在安全对齐和指令调优方面取得了进展，但仍然容易受到越狱攻击。现有方法通常需要大量查询或缺乏几何感知的干预机制，因此需要开发更高效、基于解释性的攻击方法。

Method: HMNS采用电路级干预：1) 识别对模型默认行为最具因果责任的注意力头；2) 通过定向列掩码抑制这些头的写入路径；3) 在抑制子空间的正交补空间中注入扰动。该方法采用闭环检测-干预循环，在多次解码尝试中重新识别因果头并重新应用干预。

Result: 在多个越狱基准测试、强安全防御和广泛使用的语言模型上，HMNS实现了最先进的攻击成功率，且比先前方法需要更少的查询。消融实验证实零空间约束注入、残差范数缩放和迭代重新识别是其有效性的关键。

Conclusion: HMNS是首个利用几何感知、解释性指导干预的越狱方法，为受控模型引导和对抗性安全规避开辟了新范式，突显了基于模型内部表示的针对性攻击的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks -- inputs designed to bypass safety mechanisms and elicit harmful responses -- despite advances in alignment and instruction tuning. We propose Head-Masked Nullspace Steering (HMNS), a circuit-level intervention that (i) identifies attention heads most causally responsible for a model's default behavior, (ii) suppresses their write paths via targeted column masking, and (iii) injects a perturbation constrained to the orthogonal complement of the muted subspace. HMNS operates in a closed-loop detection-intervention cycle, re-identifying causal heads and reapplying interventions across multiple decoding attempts. Across multiple jailbreak benchmarks, strong safety defenses, and widely used language models, HMNS attains state-of-the-art attack success rates with fewer queries than prior methods. Ablations confirm that nullspace-constrained injection, residual norm scaling, and iterative re-identification are key to its effectiveness. To our knowledge, this is the first jailbreak method to leverage geometry-aware, interpretability-informed interventions, highlighting a new paradigm for controlled model steering and adversarial safety circumvention.

</details>


### [96] [AI Identification: An Integrated Framework for Sustainable Governance in Digital Enterprises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10473)
*Di Kevin Gao,Jingdao Chen,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个结合技术和治理机制的AI系统识别框架，通过双标识符、区块链注册和零知识证明等技术实现AI系统的生命周期可追溯性和监管合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统变得更强大、自主并嵌入关键基础设施，其识别和可追溯性成为监管监督和可持续数字治理的基础。数字化企业的长期可持续性依赖于透明、可问责且具有生命周期治理的AI系统，这都需要可验证的身份。

Method: 提出一个概念和架构框架，整合五个组件：模型指纹、密码哈希、基于区块链的注册、基于零知识证明的所有权证明、以及部署后结构变化筛查。引入双层级标识符（机器可验证的主哈希和人类可读的次标识符），锚定在防篡改注册表中。身份验证通过治理定义的检查点进行选择性零知识证明验证，部署后变化使用Lempel-Ziv Jaccard距离作为治理导向的筛查信号。

Result: 建立了一个可执行且透明的身份基础设施，能够在AI系统生命周期中实现连续性、可审计性和政策对齐的监督。该框架将AI识别嵌入企业架构和治理流程，支持可持续创新，加强机构问责制。

Conclusion: 该框架为数字化转型期间的选择性、政策定义的验证提供了基础，通过技术治理机制确保AI系统的可追溯性和监管合规，促进可持续的数字治理。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems grow more powerful, autonomous, and embedded in critical infrastructure, their identification and traceability become foundational to regulatory oversight and sustainable digital governance. In digitally transformed enterprises, long-term sustainability depends on transparent, accountable, and lifecycle-governed AI systems, all of which require verifiable identity. This study proposes a conceptual and architectural framework for AI identification, combining technical and governance mechanisms to support lifecycle accountability. The framework integrates five components: model fingerprinting, cryptographic hashing, blockchain-based registration, zero-knowledge proof (ZKP)-based proof of possession, and post-deployment structural change screening. We introduce a dual-layer identifier, consisting of a machine-verifiable primary hash and a human-readable secondary identifier, anchored in a tamper-resistant registry. Identity validation is supported by selective ZKP-based verification at governance-defined checkpoints, while post-deployment changes are monitored using Lempel--Ziv Jaccard Distance (LZJD) as a governance-oriented screening signal rather than a semantic performance metric. The framework establishes an enforceable and transparent identity infrastructure that enables continuity, auditability, and policy-aligned oversight across AI system lifecycles. By embedding AI identification within enterprise architecture and governance processes, the proposed approach supports sustainable innovation, strengthens institutional accountability, and provides a foundation for selective, policy-defined verification during digital transformation.

</details>


### [97] [Public Key Encryption from High-Corruption Constraint Satisfaction Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10479)
*Isaac M Hair,Amit Sahai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于高腐化率约束满足问题(CSP)的公钥加密方案，具有准指数级安全性，核心是新的标记扩展因子图陷阱门方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CSP的加密方案难以同时实现高腐化率和超准多项式安全级别，需要新的构造方法

Method: 1) 提出LARP-CSP和kXOR问题的新硬度猜想；2) 基于标记扩展因子图设计加密陷阱门；3) 构造具有扩展性、低密度生成矩阵且能高效解码的纠错码

Result: 首次实现同时利用高腐化率CSP并达到远高于准多项式的安全级别，为基于CSP的加密提供了新的安全基准

Conclusion: 通过新的硬度猜想和标记扩展因子图方法，成功构建了具有准指数级安全性的公钥加密方案，为后量子密码学提供了新方向

Abstract: We give a public key encryption scheme with plausible quasi-exponential security based on the conjectured intractability of two constraint satisfaction problems (CSPs), both of which are instantiated with a corruption rate of $1 - o(1)$. First, we conjecture the hardness of a new large alphabet random predicate CSP (LARP-CSP) defined over an arbitrary but strongly expanding factor graph, where the vast majority of predicate outputs are replaced with random outputs. Second, we conjecture the hardness of the standard $k$XOR problem defined over a random factor graph, again where the vast majority of parity computations are replaced with random bits. In support of our hardness conjecture for LARP-CSPs, we give a variety of lower bounds, ruling out many natural attacks including all known attacks that exploit non-random factor graphs.
  Our public key encryption scheme is the first to leverage high corruption CSPs while simultaneously achieving a plausible security level far above quasi-polynomial. At the heart of our work is a new method for planting cryptographic trapdoors based on the label extended factor graph for a CSP.
  Along the way to achieving our result, we give the first uniform construction of an error-correcting code that has an expanding, low density generator matrix while simultaneously allowing for efficient decoding from a $1 - o(1)$ fraction of corruptions.

</details>


### [98] [SEED: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Provenance Tracing in Sequential Deepfake Facial Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10522)
*Mengieong Hoi,Zhedong Zheng,Ping Liu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SEED是一个用于面部图像序列溯源追踪的大规模基准数据集，包含90K+通过扩散模型进行1-4步顺序编辑的图像，支持伪造检测和序列预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的深度伪造内容通常经过多次顺序编辑，现有数据集主要关注单步编辑，忽略了真实多步编辑管道引入的累积伪影，需要专门的数据集来研究序列溯源追踪。

Method: 提出SEED基准数据集，包含通过扩散模型进行顺序属性编辑生成的图像，提供编辑顺序、文本指令、操作掩码和生成模型等细粒度标注。同时提出FAITH基线方法，结合空间和频域线索（特别是小波分量）来识别和排序潜在编辑事件。

Result: 评估显示仅依赖空间信息的方法在扩散模型产生的细微和分布式伪影下表现不佳，特别是在多步编辑累积伪影时。FAITH方法通过结合频域信号（特别是高频小波分量）即使在图像退化情况下也能提供有效线索。

Conclusion: SEED为AI生成视觉内容的序列溯源追踪和证据聚合提供了系统研究平台，高频信号（特别是小波分量）是识别顺序编辑的有效线索，有助于可信的视觉内容分析。

Abstract: Deepfake content on social networks is increasingly produced through multiple \emph{sequential} edits to biometric data such as facial imagery. Consequently, the final appearance of an image often reflects a latent chain of operations rather than a single manipulation. Recovering these editing histories is essential for visual provenance analysis, misinformation auditing, and forensic or platform moderation workflows that must trace the origin and evolution of AI-generated media. However, existing datasets predominantly focus on single-step editing and overlook the cumulative artifacts introduced by realistic multi-step pipelines. To address this gap, we introduce Sequential Editing in Diffusion (\textbf{SEED}), a large-scale benchmark for sequential provenance tracing in facial imagery. SEED contains over 90K images constructed via one to four sequential attribute edits using diffusion-based editing pipelines, with fine-grained annotations including edit order, textual instructions, manipulation masks, and generation models. These metadata enable step-wise evidence analysis and support forgery detection, sequence prediction. To benchmark the challenges posed by SEED, we evaluate representative analysis strategies and observe that spatial-only approaches struggle under subtle and distributed diffusion artifacts, especially when such artifacts accumulate across multiple edits. Motivated by this observation, we further establish \textbf{FAITH}, a frequency-aware Transformer baseline that aggregates spatial and frequency-domain cues to identify and order latent editing events. Results show that high-frequency signals, particularly wavelet components, provide effective cues even under image degradation. Overall, SEED facilitates systematic study of sequential provenance tracing and evidence aggregation for trustworthy analysis of AI-generated visual content.

</details>


### [99] [Machine Learning-Based Detection of MCP Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10534)
*Tobias Mattsson,Samuel Nyberg,Anton Borg,Ricardo Britto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估了多种监督机器学习方法，用于检测恶意MCP工具描述，在二分类任务中达到100% F1分数，在多分类任务中SVC和BERT模型表现最佳，优于传统规则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP技术扩展了大型语言模型功能，但引入了新的攻击面。尽管已有研究指出相关安全漏洞，但MCP攻击检测领域仍缺乏深入探索。本研究旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 开发并评估了多种监督机器学习方法，包括传统模型和深度学习模型。在两个场景下评估：1) 二分类任务区分恶意与良性工具；2) 多分类任务识别攻击类型并区分良性工具。同时比较了作为基线的规则方法。

Result: 多个模型在二分类任务中达到100% F1分数。在多分类场景中，SVC和BERT模型表现最佳，分别达到90.56%和88.33%的F1分数。混淆矩阵提供了传统指标未捕捉的预测分布洞察。开发了中间件在实时环境中分类MCP工具安全性。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型在区分恶意和良性数据点方面表现优异，能够超越当前领域使用的传统规则解决方案。该研究为MCP防御领域提供了有价值的补充，展示了机器学习在安全检测中的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is a new and emerging technology that extends the functionality of large language models, improving workflows but also exposing users to a new attack surface. Several studies have highlighted related security flaws, but MCP attack detection remains underexplored. To address this research gap, this study develops and evaluates a range of supervised machine learning approaches, including both traditional and deep-learning models. We evaluated the systems on the detection of malicious MCP tool descriptions in two scenarios: (1) a binary classification task distinguishing malicious from benign tools, and (2) a multiclass classification task identifying the attack type while separating benign from malicious tools. In addition to the machine learning models, we compared a rule-based approach that serves as a baseline. The results indicate that several of the developed models achieved 100\% F1-score on the binary classification task. In the multiclass scenario, the SVC and BERT models performed best, achieving F1 scores of 90.56\% and 88.33\%, respectively. Confusion matrices were also used to visualize the full distribution of predictions often missed by traditional metrics, providing additional insight for selecting the best-fitting solution in real-world scenarios. This study presents an addition to the MCP defence area, showing that machine learning models can perform exceptionally well in separating malicious and benign data points. To apply the solution in a live environment, a middleware was developed to classify which MCP tools are safe to use before execution, and block the ones that are not safe. Furthermore, the study shows that these models can outperform traditional rule-based solutions currently in use in the field.

</details>


### [100] [DuCodeMark: Dual-Purpose Code Dataset Watermarking via Style-Aware Watermark-Poison Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10611)
*Yuchen Chen,Yuan Xiao,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Baowen Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DuCodeMark是一种用于代码数据集的双用途水印方法，可在源代码和反编译任务中通用，通过AST解析和风格转换构建隐蔽的触发-目标对，并注入可抑制的污染特征来增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代码大模型和开源贡献的普及，源代码数据集未经授权使用的问题日益严重。现有水印方法依赖于可检测的触发-目标模式，且仅限于源代码任务，无法覆盖反编译等其他场景。

Method: 将代码样本解析为抽象语法树，应用语言特定的风格转换构建隐蔽的触发-目标对，在返回类型样本的子集中注入可抑制的污染特征。这些特征在正常训练中保持非活跃状态，但在水印移除时被激活以降低模型性能。使用基于独立样本t检验的黑盒方法进行验证。

Result: 在72个设置（2种代码任务、2种编程语言、3个代码大模型、6个解码温度）的全面评估中，DuCodeMark表现出：强可验证性（p<0.05）、高隐蔽性（可疑率≤0.36）、对水印和污染攻击的鲁棒性（召回率≤0.57），水印移除时模型性能显著下降（Pass@1下降28.6%）。

Conclusion: DuCodeMark是一种实用且具有弹性的双用途水印方法，能够跨源代码和反编译任务通用，通过隐蔽的触发-目标对和可抑制的污染特征实现强可验证性、高隐蔽性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The proliferation of large language models for code (CodeLMs) and open-source contributions has heightened concerns over unauthorized use of source code datasets. While watermarking provides a viable protection mechanism by embedding ownership signals, existing methods rely on detectable trigger-target patterns and are limited to source-code tasks, overlooking other scenarios such as decompilation tasks. In this paper, we propose DuCodeMark, a stealthy and robust dual-purpose watermarking method for code datasets that generalizes across both source-code tasks and decompilation tasks. DuCodeMark parses each code sample into an abstract syntax tree (AST), applies language-specific style transformations to construct stealthy trigger-target pairs, and injects repressible poisoned features into a subset of return-typed samples to enhance robustness against watermark removal or evasion. These features remain inactive during normal training but are activated upon watermark removal, degrading model performance. For verification, DuCodeMark employs a black-box method based on the independent-samples $t$-test. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of DuCodeMark across 72 settings spanning two code tasks, two programming languages, three CodeLMs, and six decoding temperatures. The results demonstrate that it consistently achieves strong verifiability ($p < 0.05$), high stealthiness (suspicious rate $\leq$ 0.36), robustness against both watermark and poisoning attacks (recall $\leq$ 0.57), and a substantial drop in model performance upon watermark removal (Pass@1 drops by 28.6%), underscoring its practicality and resilience.

</details>


### [101] [Analyzing Vector Register Usage in Linux Packages to Understand Real-World Impact of Downfall Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10648)
*Yohei Harata,Soramichi Akiyama*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析Ubuntu LTS版本中超过13.3万个软件包的向量寄存器使用情况，评估Downfall侧信道攻击对日常计算活动的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Downfall侧信道攻击能够泄露同一CPU核心上进程间的向量寄存器值，可能导致严重后果（如窃取AES密钥）。虽然原始论文和Intel已讨论其影响，但尚不清楚普通用户日常计算活动中使用的程序是否受Downfall影响

Method: 收集Ubuntu四个最新长期支持版本提供的所有软件包（超过13.3万个），测量向量指令的各种指标，分析向量寄存器在广泛使用应用程序中的使用情况

Result: 超过60%的二进制文件至少使用一个向量寄存器；一些高度流行的软件包（如apt）也可能受Downfall影响

Conclusion: Downfall攻击对日常计算活动具有实际影响，需要更广泛的安全评估和缓解措施

Abstract: Downfall is a side-channel attack that leaks values in vector registers from a process to another on the same CPU core. This attack enables an attacker to achieve serious outcomes (e.g., stealing AES keys), and there is no fundamental countermeasure besides applying microcode-based hardware patches. Although the impact of this attack is discussed by the original paper and by Intel to some extent, it is still unclear whether programs used in daily computing activities of normal users are affected by Downfall. This paper thoroughly analyzes the usage of vector registers in widely used applications to assess the impact of Downfall on them. In particular, we collect all packages (over 133~K) provided by the four latest long-term support versions of Ubuntu and measure various metrics on vector instructions. Our findings include that over 60% of all binary files contained in the packages use at least one vector register, and that some highly popular packages such as apt might also be affected by Downfall.

</details>


### [102] [Critical-CoT: A Robust Defense Framework against Reasoning-Level Backdoor Attacks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10681)
*Vu Tuan Truong,Long Bao Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Critical-CoT：针对LLMs推理级后门攻击的两阶段微调防御机制，通过培养批判性思维使模型自动识别并拒绝生成恶意推理步骤


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击防御主要针对token级攻击，而针对推理级后门攻击（在CoT中插入恶意推理步骤）的防御研究不足，这类攻击更难检测因为后门答案在中毒推理轨迹中仍然合理一致

Method: 提出Critical-CoT防御机制，通过两阶段微调过程：第一阶段训练模型识别推理步骤中的逻辑缺陷和矛盾，第二阶段训练模型拒绝生成包含潜在后门的推理步骤，从而培养LLMs的批判性思维能力

Result: 在多个LLMs和数据集上的实验表明，Critical-CoT对基于上下文学习和基于微调的后门攻击都具有强鲁棒性，且表现出良好的跨领域和跨任务泛化能力

Conclusion: Critical-CoT是首个针对LLMs推理级后门攻击的有效防御机制，通过培养批判性思维使模型能够自动检测并拒绝恶意推理步骤，为LLMs安全提供了新解决方案

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), despite their impressive capabilities across domains, have been shown to be vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Prior backdoor strategies predominantly operate at the token level, where an injected trigger causes the model to generate a specific target word, choice, or class (depending on the task). Recent advances, however, exploit the long-form reasoning tendencies of modern LLMs to conduct reasoning-level backdoors: once triggered, the victim model inserts one or more malicious reasoning steps into its chain-of-thought (CoT). These attacks are substantially harder to detect, as the backdoored answer remains plausible and consistent with the poisoned reasoning trajectory. Yet, defenses tailored to this type of backdoor remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Critical-CoT, a novel defense mechanism that conducts a two-stage fine-tuning (FT) process on LLMs to develop critical thinking behaviors, enabling them to automatically identify potential backdoors and refuse to generate malicious reasoning steps. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and datasets demonstrate that Critical-CoT provides strong robustness against both in-context learning-based and FT-based backdoor attacks. Notably, Critical-CoT exhibits strong cross-domain and cross-task generalization. Our code is available at hthttps://github.com/tuanvu171/Critical-CoT.

</details>


### [103] [COD-ssi: Enforcing Mutual Privacy for Credential Oblivious Disclosure in Self Sovereign Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10685)
*Elia Onofri,Andrea De Salve,Paolo Mori,Laura Emilia Maria Ricci,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: COD-ssi框架首次在SSI中实现双向隐私保护，使用OPRF技术让验证者能选择性访问声明而不向持有者透露具体访问了哪些声明


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SSI的选择性披露机制主要保护持有者隐私，但验证者的隐私保护不足。验证者请求的特定凭证信息可能无意中向持有者泄露敏感信息，如内部决策标准、业务规则或战略计划

Method: 提出COD-ssi（SSI声明无感知披露）框架，利用Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions（OPRF）技术，使验证者能选择性访问声明子集，同时不向凭证持有者透露具体访问了哪些声明

Result: 安全性得到形式化验证，通过开源原型实现的实验评估证实可行性。结果表明，在SSI中实现可证明的双向隐私保护仅需适度的计算和通信开销

Conclusion: COD-ssi首次在SSI中实现了双向隐私保护，解决了验证者隐私泄露问题，为SSI生态系统提供了更全面的隐私保护方案

Abstract: The Self-Sovereign Identity (SSI) paradigm is instrumental for decentralised identity management, allowing an entity to create, manage, and present their digital credentials without relying on centralised authorities. Credential selective disclosure is one of the most attractive privacy-preserving features of SSI, allowing users to reveal only the minimum necessary information from their credentials. However, current selective disclosure mechanisms primarily focus on protecting the privacy of credential Holders, while offering limited protection to the Verifiers of credentials. Indeed, the specific credential information requested by a Verifier can inadvertently reveal to credential Holders sensitive information, including internal decision-making criteria, business rules, or strategic plans. In this work, we address this threat by proposing, to the best of our knowledge, the first approach that enforces mutual privacy in credential exchanges. To this end, we introduce COD-ssi (Claim Oblivious Disclosure for SSI), a novel framework that leverages Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions to allow Verifiers to selectively access a subset of claims without revealing which specific claims were accessed to the credential Holder. The security of our solution is formally verified and its feasibility is assessed through the experimental evaluation of our open-source prototype implementation. These results show that provable mutual privacy in the context of SSI can be achieved with just moderate computational and communication overhead.

</details>


### [104] [Detecting RAG Extraction Attack via Dual-Path Runtime Integrity Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10717)
*Yuanbo Xie,Yingjie Zhang,Yulin Li,Shouyou Song,Xiaokun Chen,Zhihan Liu,Liya Su,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CanaryRAG：一种基于栈金丝雀原理的运行时防御机制，通过嵌入金丝雀令牌来检测和防止RAG系统中的知识库泄露攻击，无需重新训练即可集成到现有RAG管道中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽然增强了大型语言模型的外部知识获取能力，但引入了关键的安全漏洞：RAG知识库泄露。攻击者可以通过自适应迭代攻击策略诱导模型泄露检索到的专有内容，而目前缺乏有效的防御措施。

Method: 提出CanaryRAG防御机制，受软件安全中栈金丝雀启发，在检索到的文本块中嵌入精心设计的金丝雀令牌，将RAG提取防御重新定义为双路径运行时完整性游戏。当目标路径或预言机路径违反预期的金丝雀行为时（包括自适应抑制和混淆），实时检测泄露。

Result: 针对现有攻击的广泛评估表明，CanaryRAG提供鲁棒防御，相比最先进的基线方法显著降低了块恢复率，同时对任务性能和推理延迟的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: CanaryRAG作为一种即插即用解决方案，无需重新训练或结构修改即可无缝集成到任意RAG管道中，为专有数据提供了实用且可扩展的安全保障。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems augment large language models with external knowledge, yet introduce a critical security vulnerability: RAG Knowledge Base Leakage, wherein adversarial prompts can induce the model to divulge retrieved proprietary content. Recent studies reveal that such leakage can be executed through adaptive and iterative attack strategies (named RAG extraction attack), while effective countermeasures remain notably lacking. To bridge this gap, we propose CanaryRAG, a runtime defense mechanism inspired by stack canaries in software security. CanaryRAG embeds carefully designed canary tokens into retrieved chunks and reformulates RAG extraction defense as a dual-path runtime integrity game. Leakage is detected in real time whenever either the target or oracle path violates its expected canary behavior, including under adaptive suppression and obfuscation. Extensive evaluations against existing attacks demonstrate that CanaryRAG provides robust defense, achieving substantially lower chunk recovery rates than state-of-the-art baselines while imposing negligible impact on task performance and inference latency. Moreover, as a plug-and-play solution, CanaryRAG can be seamlessly integrated into arbitrary RAG pipelines without requiring retraining or structural modifications, offering a practical and scalable safeguard for proprietary data.

</details>


### [105] [Privacy as Permissible Operations: An ABAC Framework for Policy-Law Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10832)
*Ajay Dhakar,Arunesh Sinha,Shamik Sural*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: APLiance是一个基于ABAC的框架，用于检查组织隐私政策是否符合相关法律要求，特别针对印度《数字个人数据保护法》进行了验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着各国制定个人数据隐私保护法律，企业隐私政策需要符合法律要求，但缺乏有效的自动化检查方法

Method: 将隐私法律条款建模为基于属性的访问控制（ABAC）规则，将隐私政策条款建模为隐含的访问请求序列，通过检查访问请求是否被ABAC规则允许来判断合规性

Result: 开发了APLiance框架，并针对印度《数字个人数据保护法》进行了验证，创建了浏览器插件用于实时合规检查

Conclusion: APLiance提供了一种有效的方法来自动检查隐私政策是否符合法律要求，有助于确保企业遵守数据保护法规

Abstract: In recent years, many countries have started enacting laws to safeguard privacy of personal data of their citizens collected and maintained by various enterprises through websites, mobile apps, and other means. It is imperative that the privacy policies of these enterprises respect the provisions of the applicable law. In this paper, we show how such organizational privacy policies can be efficiently checked against a prevalent law. Our novel approach named APLiance (\underline{A}BAC framework for \underline{P}olicy-\underline{L}aw Compl\underline{iance}) models the requirements of the different sections of a privacy law in the form of Attribute-based Access Control (ABAC) rules and the clauses of a privacy policy as a sequence of implied access requests. A policy is considered to be compliant with the law if these access requests are permitted by the corresponding ABAC rules. Although APLiance can be used in any policy-law setting, we demonstrate its effectiveness in the context of the recently introduced Digital Personal Data Protection Act of India. A browser plugin has been developed and publicly released for real time compliance checking using APLiance whenever a user visits the privacy policy page of a website.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond A Fixed Seal: Adaptive Stealing Watermark in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10893)
*Shuhao Zhang,Yuli Chen,Jiale Han,Bo Cheng,Jiabao Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为自适应窃取（AS）的新型水印窃取算法，通过位置基础密封构建和自适应选择模块增强设计灵活性，显著提高了对目标水印的窃取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印窃取算法（SWAs）采用固定策略，忽略了窃取水印信息的非均匀分布和真实世界LLM生成过程的动态特性，导致攻击效果有限。需要更灵活的自适应方法来应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出自适应窃取（AS）算法，包含位置基础密封构建和自适应选择两个核心模块。AS通过定义基于上下文有序令牌不同激活状态的多个攻击视角，在攻击执行时根据水印兼容性、生成优先级和动态生成相关性动态选择最优攻击视角。

Result: 实验表明，在相同实验条件下，AS显著提高了对目标水印的窃取效率，相比现有方法具有更好的攻击效果。

Conclusion: AS算法的成功揭示了当前LLM水印的脆弱性，强调了需要开发更强大的水印技术来抵御潜在攻击。作者开源代码以促进未来研究。

Abstract: Watermarking provides a critical safeguard for large language model (LLM) services by facilitating the detection of LLM-generated text. Correspondingly, stealing watermark algorithms (SWAs) derive watermark information from watermarked texts generated by victim LLMs to craft highly targeted adversarial attacks, which compromise the reliability of watermarks. Existing SWAs rely on fixed strategies, overlooking the non-uniform distribution of stolen watermark information and the dynamic nature of real-world LLM generation processes. To address these limitations, we propose Adaptive Stealing (AS), a novel SWA featuring enhanced design flexibility through Position-Based Seal Construction and Adaptive Selection modules. AS operates by defining multiple attack perspectives derived from distinct activation states of contextually ordered tokens. During attack execution, AS dynamically selects the optimal perspective based on watermark compatibility, generation priority, and dynamic generation relevance. Our experiments demonstrate that AS significantly increases steal efficiency against target watermarks under identical experimental conditions. These findings highlight the need for more robust LLM watermarks to withstand potential attacks. We release our code to the community for future research\footnote{https://github.com/DrankXs/AdaptiveStealingWatermark}.

</details>


### [107] [Hardware-Efficient Compound IC Protection with Lightweight Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11148)
*Levent Aksoy,Muhammad Sohaib Munir,Sedat Akleylek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种结合轻量级密码算法、逻辑锁定和硬件混淆的复合IC保护机制，相比现有密码算法方案显著降低硬件复杂度，并能抵抗多种攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IC保护技术大多缺乏可证明安全算法，效率低下；使用密码算法的方案硬件复杂度高，易受移除攻击和功耗分析攻击。

Method: 提出复合IC保护机制，结合轻量级密码算法、逻辑锁定和硬件混淆技术，开发相应工具生成安全设计。

Result: 实验结果显示，所生成的安全设计相比现有密码算法方案硬件复杂度显著降低，能抵抗移除攻击、代数攻击和逻辑锁定攻击。

Conclusion: 该复合保护机制在保证安全性的同时有效降低了硬件复杂度，为IC供应链安全提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Over the years, many techniques have been introduced to protect integrated circuits (ICs) from hardware security threats that emerged in the globalized IC manufacturing supply chain, such as overproduction and piracy. However, most of these techniques have been rendered inefficient since they do not rely on provably secure algorithms. Moreover, the previously proposed techniques using cryptography algorithms lead to a significant increase in hardware complexity and are vulnerable to the removal and power analysis attacks. In this paper, we propose a compound IC protection mechanism that uses a lightweight cryptography algorithm with prominent logic locking and hardware obfuscation techniques. Experimental results show that the secure designs generated by the developed tool have significantly less hardware complexity when compared to those generated by previously proposed techniques using cryptography algorithms and are resilient to existing removal, algebraic, and logic locking attacks.

</details>


### [108] [The Salami Slicing Threat: Exploiting Cumulative Risks in LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11309)
*Yihao Zhang,Kai Wang,Jiangrong Wu,Haolin Wu,Yuxuan Zhou,Zeming Wei,Dongxian Wu,Xun Chen,Jun Sun,Meng Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出"萨拉米切片风险"概念和"Salami Attack"自动框架，通过串联多个低风险输入来累积有害意图，最终触发高风险行为，在多轮越狱攻击中实现了超过90%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮越狱攻击方法存在两个根本性局限：1) 随着模型上下文感知能力增强，任何显式有害触发词都容易被标记和阻止；2) 成功的最终步骤触发通常需要精细调整的模型特定上下文，使得攻击高度依赖上下文。需要一种不严重依赖预设计上下文结构的新型攻击方法。

Method: 提出"Salami Attack"自动框架，基于"萨拉米切片风险"概念，通过串联大量低风险输入来累积有害意图，每个输入单独都能规避对齐阈值，但累积起来最终触发高风险行为。该方法适用于多种模型类型和模态，不严重依赖预设计的上下文结构。

Result: 在GPT-4o和Gemini等多样化模型和模态上实现了超过90%的攻击成功率，并展示了对现实世界对齐防御的鲁棒性。同时提出了防御策略，能将Salami Attack约束至少44.8%，对其他多轮越狱攻击的最大阻断率达到64.8%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多轮越狱攻击的普遍风险，提供了可行的缓解策略来增强LLM安全性。Salami Attack展示了通过低风险输入累积实现越狱的有效性，为理解和防御这类攻击提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) face prominent security risks from jailbreaking, a practice that manipulates models to bypass built-in security constraints and generate unethical or unsafe content. Among various jailbreak techniques, multi-turn jailbreak attacks are more covert and persistent than single-turn counterparts, exposing critical vulnerabilities of LLMs.
  However, existing multi-turn jailbreak methods suffer from two fundamental limitations that affect the actual impact in real-world scenarios: (a) As models become more context-aware, any explicit harmful trigger is increasingly likely to be flagged and blocked; (b) Successful final-step triggers often require finely tuned, model-specific contexts, making such attacks highly context-dependent. To fill this gap, we propose \textit{Salami Slicing Risk}, which operates by chaining numerous low-risk inputs that individually evade alignment thresholds but cumulatively accumulate harmful intent to ultimately trigger high-risk behaviors, without heavy reliance on pre-designed contextual structures. Building on this risk, we develop Salami Attack, an automatic framework universally applicable to multiple model types and modalities.
  Rigorous experiments demonstrate its state-of-the-art performance across diverse models and modalities, achieving over 90\% Attack Success Rate on GPT-4o and Gemini, as well as robustness against real-world alignment defenses. We also proposed a defense strategy to constrain the Salami Attack by at least 44.8\% while achieving a maximum blocking rate of 64.8\% against other multi-turn jailbreak attacks. Our findings provide critical insights into the pervasive risks of multi-turn jailbreaking and offer actionable mitigation strategies to enhance LLM security.

</details>


### [109] [Geometry-Aware Localized Watermarking for Copyright Protection in Embedding-as-a-Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11344)
*Zhimin Chen,Xiaojie Liang,Wenbo Xu,Yuxuan Liu,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GeoMark是一个几何感知的局部水印框架，用于解决EaaS版权保护中的鲁棒性-实用性-可验证性矛盾，通过几何分离锚点和自适应局部邻域实现局部触发和集中归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EaaS水印方法面临鲁棒性-实用性-可验证性的根本矛盾：基于触发的方法对改写敏感，基于变换的方法对维度扰动敏感，基于区域的方法可能因偶然几何亲和性产生误报。

Method: GeoMark使用自然流形内嵌入作为共享水印目标，构建具有明确目标-锚点边界的几何分离锚点，并仅在自适应局部邻域内激活水印注入，实现局部触发和集中归属的解耦。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，GeoMark在保持下游实用性和几何保真度的同时，在改写、维度扰动和CSE攻击下保持鲁棒的版权验证，具有改进的验证稳定性和低误报风险。

Conclusion: GeoMark通过几何感知的局部水印框架有效解决了EaaS版权保护中的鲁棒性-实用性-可验证性矛盾，为模型窃取和版权侵权提供了有效的防护方案。

Abstract: Embedding-as-a-Service (EaaS) has become an important semantic infrastructure for natural language and multimedia applications, but it is highly vulnerable to model stealing and copyright infringement. Existing EaaS watermarking methods face a fundamental robustness--utility--verifiability tension: trigger-based methods are fragile to paraphrasing, transformation-based methods are sensitive to dimensional perturbation, and region-based methods may incur false positives due to coincidental geometric affinity.
  To address this problem, we propose GeoMark, a geometry-aware localized watermarking framework for EaaS copyright protection. GeoMark uses a natural in-manifold embedding as a shared watermark target, constructs geometry-separated anchors with explicit target--anchor margins, and activates watermark injection only within adaptive local neighborhoods. This design decouples where watermarking is triggered from what ownership is attributed to, achieving localized triggering and centralized attribution.
  Experiments on four benchmark datasets show that GeoMark preserves downstream utility and geometric fidelity while maintaining robust copyright verification under paraphrasing, dimensional perturbation, and CSE (Clustering, Selection, Elimination) attacks, with improved verification stability and low false-positive risk.

</details>


### [110] [How to reconstruct (anonymously) a secret cellular automaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11362)
*Luca Mariot,Federico Mazzone,Luca Manzoni,Alberto Leporati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于细胞自动机的匿名秘密共享方案，通过MOLS重新定义秘密空间，实现无需参与者身份信息的秘密恢复


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于细胞自动机的秘密共享方案需要知道参与者的身份信息才能恢复秘密，这限制了应用场景。研究旨在设计一种允许匿名重建的秘密共享方案，即仅凭份额就能恢复秘密，无需参与者身份信息。

Method: 重新审视基于细胞自动机的(2,n)门限方案，将其与相互正交拉丁方(MOLS)联系起来。将秘密空间重新定义为MOLS族本身，而不是传统的秘密值。通过这种重新定义，构建的新方案能够实现秘密CA规则的匿名重建。

Result: 提出的方案成功实现了匿名秘密重建功能，秘密可以仅从份额中恢复，无需参与者身份信息。同时分析了方案中可共享的秘密CA数量与恢复阶段计算复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 基于细胞自动机和MOLS的重新定义，成功构建了支持匿名重建的门限秘密共享方案。该方案在匿名性和功能性之间取得了平衡，为需要隐私保护的分布式系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider threshold secret sharing schemes based on cellular automata (CA) that allows for anonymous reconstruction, meaning that the secret can be recovered only as a function of the shares, without knowing the participants' identities. To this end, we revisit the basic characterization of $(2,n)$ threshold schemes based on CA in terms of Mutually Orthogonal Latin Squares (MOLS), and redefine the secret space as the MOLS family itself, showing that the new resulting scheme enables anonymous reconstruction of secret CA rules. Finally, we discuss the trade-off between the number of secret CA that can be shared and the computational complexity of the recovery phase.

</details>


### [111] [Optimizing IoT Intrusion Detection with Tabular Foundation Models for Smart City Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11394)
*Asma Al-Dahmani,Abdulla Bin Safwan,Mohammad Obeidat,Belal Alsinglawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TabPFNv2.5在物联网入侵检测中比随机森林推理速度快40倍，同时保持97%的二元分类准确率，适合实时安全操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧城市安全操作需要平衡准确性和响应时间的检测系统，传统集成方法（如随机森林）计算开销大，阻碍实时取证分类

Method: 系统评估基于Transformer的基础模型TabPFNv2.5与传统集成分类器在物联网入侵检测中的性能，使用TON IoT数据集，并提出混合管道：TabPFNv2.5进行快速威胁筛查，集成模型处理详细分类

Result: TabPFNv2.5比随机森林推理速度快40倍，保持97%二元分类准确率；扫描攻击最难检测（F1: 69.8%）；跨设备泛化严重依赖特征相似性

Conclusion: 基础模型可作为时间敏感物联网安全操作的可行组件，混合管道方法能平衡速度和准确性需求

Abstract: Security operations in smart cities demand detection systems that balance accuracy with response time. While ensemble methods like Random Forest achieve high accuracy, their computational overhead impedes real-time forensic triage. We present the first systematic evaluation of TabPFNv2.5, a transformer-based foundation model, against traditional ensemble classifiers for IoT intrusion detection. Using the TON IoT dataset, we demonstrate that TabPFNv2.5 achieves 40 faster inference than Random Forest while maintaining 97% binary classification accuracy. We propose a hybrid pipeline in which TabPFNv2.5 performs rapid threat screening, while ensemble models handle detailed classification. Our analysis reveals that scanning attacks remain the hardest to detect (F1: 69.8%) and cross-device generalization depends critically on feature similarity. These findings establish foundation models as viable components for time-sensitive IoT security operations

</details>


### [112] [RLSpoofer: A Lightweight Evaluator for LLM Watermark Spoofing Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11546)
*Hanbo Huang,Xuan Gong,Yiran Zhang,Hao Zheng,Shiyu Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RLSpoofer，一种基于强化学习的黑盒水印欺骗攻击方法，仅需100个人工水印改写训练对，无需访问水印内部算法或检测器，就能实现62%的欺骗成功率，暴露了当前LLM水印方案的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型水印技术的黑盒欺骗鲁棒性评估不足，现有评估方法需要大量数据集和白盒访问算法内部，限制了实际应用。需要从分布角度研究水印对抗欺骗的韧性。

Method: 首先建立局部容量瓶颈理论框架，描述在KL有界局部更新下可重新分配的概率质量。基于此提出RLSpoofer，一种基于强化学习的黑盒欺骗攻击，仅需100个人工水印改写训练对，无需访问水印内部或检测器。

Result: RLSpoofer使4B模型在PF标记文本上实现62.0%的欺骗成功率，语义偏移最小，远超基线模型（使用多达10,000样本仅6%成功率）。

Conclusion: 当前LLM水印方案对欺骗攻击的抵抗能力脆弱，需要更鲁棒的水印方案。本文提供了轻量级评估框架，强调了开发更强大水印方案的紧迫性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) watermarking has emerged as a promising approach for detecting and attributing AI-generated text, yet its robustness to black-box spoofing remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing evaluation methods often demand extensive datasets and white-box access to algorithmic internals, limiting their practical applicability. In this paper, we study watermark resilience against spoofing fundamentally from a distributional perspective. We first establish a \textit{local capacity bottleneck}, which theoretically characterizes the probability mass that can be reallocated under KL-bounded local updates while preserving semantic fidelity. Building on this, we propose RLSpoofer, a reinforcement learning-based black-box spoofing attack that requires only 100 human-watermarked paraphrase training pairs and zero access to the watermarking internals or detectors. Despite weak supervision, it empowers a 4B model to achieve a 62.0\% spoof success rate with minimal semantic shift on PF-marked texts, dwarfing the 6\% of baseline models trained on up to 10,000 samples. Our findings expose the fragile spoofing resistance of current LLM watermarking paradigms, providing a lightweight evaluation framework and stressing the urgent need for more robust schemes.

</details>


### [113] [AmBox: Device-to-Blockchain Ambient Sensing for Food Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11681)
*João Miguel Guerreiro Fernandes,Samih Eisa,Miguel L. Pardal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AmBox系统通过将环境传感器连接到区块链，为食品追溯提供安全、可验证、防篡改的数据采集，减少中间环节，增强供应链透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品供应链中环境条件监测对确保质量和可追溯性至关重要，但现有环境传感设备收集的数据在利益相关者之间难以保证完整性，需要解决数据安全验证和防篡改问题。

Method: 开发AmBox系统，将传感器连接到区块链，支持独立节点或分布式节点-终端架构部署，使用Raspberry Pi和ESP32硬件原型，在Hyperledger Fabric上直接记录传感器数据。

Result: 实验结果表明AmBox能够提供及时可靠的数据，增加供应链利益相关者之间的透明度和信任，实现安全、可验证、防篡改的环境数据采集。

Conclusion: AmBox系统通过设备到区块链的环境传感，为食品可追溯性提供了有效的解决方案，确保数据完整性并减少中间环节，可在供应链不同节点灵活部署。

Abstract: From production to consumption, ensuring food quality and traceability depends on reliable monitoring of environmental conditions across the supply chain. Ambient sensing devices can collect relevant data such as temperature and humidity, but ensuring its integrity among stakeholders remains a challenge. This work presents AmBox, a system that enables device-to-blockchain ambient sensing for food traceability. AmBox connects sensors to a blockchain, ensuring secure, verifiable, and tamper-resistant data collection with minimal intermediaries. It manages sensor commissioning and operation with the adequate business context. AmBox can operate with standalone nodes or within a distributed node-mote architecture, allowing flexible deployment at different points along the supply chain. A prototype using Raspberry Pi and ESP32 hardware can record sensor data directly on Hyperledger Fabric. Experimental results show that AmBox provides timely and reliable data that can increase transparency and trust between the supply chain stakeholders.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [LABBench2: An Improved Benchmark for AI Systems Performing Biology Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09554)
*Jon M Laurent,Albert Bou,Michael Pieler,Conor Igoe,Alex Andonian,Siddharth Narayanan,James Braza,Alexandros Sanchez Vassopoulos,Jacob L Steenwyk,Blake Lash,Andrew D White,Samuel G Rodriques*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LABBench2是LAB-Bench基准的进化版本，包含近1900个任务，在更真实的情境中测量AI系统执行科学任务的能力，难度显著提高（模型准确率下降26%-46%），继续作为AI科学研究能力的实际基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI加速科学发现的乐观情绪增长，需要从测量基础知识和推理能力转向测量AI系统执行实际有意义科学工作的能力。LAB-Bench2旨在更真实地评估AI在科学领域的实际应用能力。

Method: LABBench2是LAB-Bench基准的进化版本，包含近1900个任务，大部分延续LAB-Bench的能力测量，但在更真实的情境中进行评估。提供了任务数据集和公开评估框架。

Result: 评估当前前沿模型显示，虽然LAB-Bench和LABBench2测量的能力都有显著提升，但LABBench2提供了有意义的难度跳跃（模型特定准确率在不同子任务中下降26%到46%），表明性能仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: LABBench2延续了LAB-Bench作为AI科学研究能力实际基准的传统，有助于推动AI工具在核心研究功能方面的发展。提供了公开数据集和评估框架以促进社区使用和发展。

Abstract: Optimism for accelerating scientific discovery with AI continues to grow. Current applications of AI in scientific research range from training dedicated foundation models on scientific data to agentic autonomous hypothesis generation systems to AI-driven autonomous labs. The need to measure progress of AI systems in scientific domains correspondingly must not only accelerate, but increasingly shift focus to more real-world capabilities. Beyond rote knowledge and even just reasoning to actually measuring the ability to perform meaningful work. Prior work introduced the Language Agent Biology Benchmark LAB-Bench as an initial attempt at measuring these abilities. Here we introduce an evolution of that benchmark, LABBench2, for measuring real-world capabilities of AI systems performing useful scientific tasks. LABBench2 comprises nearly 1,900 tasks and is, for the most part, a continuation of LAB-Bench, measuring similar capabilities but in more realistic contexts. We evaluate performance of current frontier models, and show that while abilities measured by LAB-Bench and LABBench2 have improved substantially, LABBench2 provides a meaningful jump in difficulty (model-specific accuracy differences range from -26% to -46% across subtasks) and underscores continued room for performance improvement. LABBench2 continues the legacy of LAB-Bench as a de facto benchmark for AI scientific research capabilities and we hope that it continues to help advance development of AI tools for these core research functions. To facilitate community use and development, we provide the task dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/futurehouse/labbench2 and a public eval harness at https://github.com/EdisonScientific/labbench2.

</details>


### [115] [Linear Programming for Multi-Criteria Assessment with Cardinal and Ordinal Data: A Pessimistic Virtual Gap Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09555)
*Fuh-Hwa Franklin Liu,Su-Chuan Shih*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于虚拟差距分析的两步法，通过悲观视角评估多准则决策中的备选方案，使用定量/定性准则和基数/序数数据，优先排除最差方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多准则分析方法存在主观评价偏差和数据多样性影响参数精度的问题，需要更可靠、可扩展的决策支持方法

Method: 提出两步法：1) 整合两种新型虚拟差距分析模型，从悲观视角评估各备选方案，处理定量/定性准则和基数/序数数据；2) 优先排序并排除最不利方案

Result: 该方法可靠且可扩展，能够在决策支持系统中高效有效地进行全面评估

Conclusion: 基于线性规划的虚拟差距分析模型能够有效解决多准则决策中的主观偏差和数据多样性问题，提供更可靠的决策支持

Abstract: Multi-criteria Analysis (MCA) is used to rank alternatives based on various criteria. Key MCA methods, such as Multiple Criteria Decision Making (MCDM) methods, estimate parameters for criteria to compute the performance of each alternative. Nonetheless, subjective evaluations and biases frequently influence the reliability of results, while the diversity of data affects the precision of the parameters. The novel linear programming-based Virtual Gap Analysis (VGA) models tackle these issues. This paper outlines a two-step method that integrates two novel VGA models to assess each alternative from a pessimistic perspective, using both quantitative and qualitative criteria, and employing cardinal and ordinal data. Next, prioritize the alternatives to eliminate the least favorable one. The proposed method is dependable and scalable, enabling thorough assessments efficiently and effectively within decision support systems.

</details>


### [116] [Seven simple steps for log analysis in AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09563)
*Magda Dubois,Ekin Zorer,Maia Hamin,Joe Skinner,Alexandra Souly,Jerome Wynne,Harry Coppock,Lucas Satos,Sayash Kapoor,Sunischal Dev,Keno Juchems,Kimberly Mai,Timo Flesch,Lennart Luettgau,Charles Teague,Eric Patey,JJ Allaire,Lorenzo Pacchiardi,Jose Hernandez-Orallo,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于当前最佳实践的AI系统日志分析标准化流程框架，提供具体代码示例和详细指导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在与工具和用户交互时产生大量日志，分析这些日志有助于理解模型能力、倾向和行为，或评估实验是否按预期进行。目前研究人员已开始开发日志分析方法，但缺乏标准化方法。

Method: 提出基于当前最佳实践的日志分析流程框架，通过Inspect Scout库提供具体代码示例，详细指导每个步骤，并强调常见陷阱。

Result: 开发了一个为研究人员提供严格且可重复日志分析基础的框架。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI系统日志分析提供了标准化方法，有助于实现严谨和可重复的研究实践。

Abstract: AI systems produce large volumes of logs as they interact with tools and users. Analysing these logs can help understand model capabilities, propensities, and behaviours, or assess whether an evaluation worked as intended. Researchers have started developing methods for log analysis, but a standardised approach is still missing. Here we suggest a pipeline based on current best practices. We illustrate it with concrete code examples in the Inspect Scout library, provide detailed guidance on each step, and highlight common pitfalls. Our framework provides researchers with a foundation for rigorous and reproducible log analysis.

</details>


### [117] [AHC: Meta-Learned Adaptive Compression for Continual Object Detection on Memory-Constrained Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09576)
*Bibin Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AHC是一种用于微控制器上持续目标检测的元学习框架，通过自适应分层压缩在100KB内存限制下实现高效特征压缩，减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存小于100KB的微控制器上部署持续目标检测需要高效的特征压缩方法。现有固定压缩策略无法适应异构任务特性，导致内存利用不佳和灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出自适应分层压缩(AHC)框架：1)基于MAML的压缩，通过5步内循环梯度下降适应新任务；2)分层多尺度压缩，根据FPN冗余模式采用不同压缩比(P3:8:1, P4:6.4:1, P5:4:1)；3)双内存架构结合短期和长期存储库，在100KB硬预算下进行基于重要性的整合。

Result: 在CORe50、TiROD和PASCAL VOC基准测试中，与三种标准基线(Fine-tuning、EWC、iCaRL)相比，AHC在100KB回放预算内实现了实用的持续检测，通过平均池化压缩特征回放结合EWC正则化和特征蒸馏获得竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: AHC框架为微控制器上的持续目标检测提供了理论保证和实际解决方案，在严格内存约束下有效管理灾难性遗忘，实现了O(ε√T + 1/√M)的遗忘上界。

Abstract: Deploying continual object detection on microcontrollers (MCUs) with under 100KB memory requires efficient feature compression that can adapt to evolving task distributions. Existing approaches rely on fixed compression strategies (e.g., FiLM conditioning) that cannot adapt to heterogeneous task characteristics, leading to suboptimal memory utilization and catastrophic forgetting. We introduce Adaptive Hierarchical Compression (AHC), a meta-learning framework featuring three key innovations: (1) true MAML-based compression that adapts via gradient descent to each new task in just 5 inner-loop steps, (2) hierarchical multi-scale compression with scale-aware ratios (8:1 for P3, 6.4:1 for P4, 4:1 for P5) matching FPN redundancy patterns, and (3) a dual-memory architecture combining short-term and long-term banks with importance-based consolidation under a hard 100KB budget. We provide formal theoretical guarantees bounding catastrophic forgetting as O(ε{sq.root(T)} + 1/{sq.root(M)}) where ε is compression error, T is task count, and M is memory size. Experiments on CORe50, TiROD, and PASCAL VOC benchmarks with three standard baselines (Fine-tuning,EWC, iCaRL) demonstrate that AHC enables practical continual detection within a 100KB replay budget, achieving competitive accuracy through mean-pooled compressed feature replay combined with EWC regularization and feature distillation.

</details>


### [118] [Explainable Planning for Hybrid Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09578)
*Mir Md Sajid Sarwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了混合系统的可解释人工智能规划（XAIP），旨在为复杂安全关键领域的AI系统生成解释


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术在智能电网、自动驾驶、医疗等安全关键领域的广泛应用，自动规划系统需要提供可解释性以满足可信度和透明度要求

Method: 论文对混合系统的可解释人工智能规划（XAIP）进行了全面研究，混合系统能够更贴近地表示现实世界问题

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体结果，但表明该研究针对混合系统的XAIP问题提供了系统性解决方案

Conclusion: 可解释性规划是AI规划社区面临的主要挑战之一，该论文为混合系统的XAIP提供了重要研究基础

Abstract: The recent advancement in artificial intelligence (AI) technologies facilitates a paradigm shift toward automation. Autonomous systems are fully or partially replacing manually crafted ones. At the core of these systems is automated planning. With the advent of powerful planners, automated planning is now applied to many complex and safety-critical domains, including smart energy grids, self-driving cars, warehouse automation, urban and air traffic control, search and rescue operations, surveillance, robotics, and healthcare. There is a growing need to generate explanations of AI-based systems, which is one of the major challenges the planning community faces today. The thesis presents a comprehensive study on explainable artificial intelligence planning (XAIP) for hybrid systems that capture a representation of real-world problems closely.

</details>


### [119] [OOWM: Structuring Embodied Reasoning and Planning via Object-Oriented Programmatic World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09580)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OOWM提出基于软件工程形式化方法的面向对象世界建模框架，用UML类图和活动图结构化具身推理，显著提升机器人规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
